- **_hash-password**: Internal helper to hash a password with Node's native scrypt (NOT bcrypt - bcrypt hashes contain $ characters that break shell and env var handling). Delegates to the bundled scripts/hash-password.mjs. Can hash a provided password or generate + hash a random one. Output format is `salt:hash` (hex:hex), shell-safe. Used by add-auth for admin credentials. Writes nothing itself; the admin's production password goes, through admin-password.mjs, to a file of the user's downloads folder for them alone, never into the conversation. Not meant to be invoked directly by users. ([flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft](https://github.com/flavien-ia/hypervibe-craft/tree/HEAD/skills/_hash-password) · ★55)

One package per invocation; leaves the change staged for the operator to review and commit. One spec per invocation; leaves the change staged for the operator to review and commit. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-demote-active-spec))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-fix-branch**: Cut one branch for fixing one or several GitHub issues, asking once which branch they belong to by listing the open feature/* branches with main as the last option. A fix for a feature in flight branches from that feature and is tested when the feature merges; a production fix with no owning feature branches from main and must pass pest before it merges, so batching several production fixes costs one pest run instead of one each. Records the target on every issue. Never writes the fix itself. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-fix-branch))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-merge**: Run the merge for one pull request, a task PR into its feature/* branch or a feature branch into main, with the gates each kind needs: check the gates with definite questions rather than inference, squash-merge on the operator's confirmation, reconcile the local machine, regenerate the documentation indexes from post-merge main, and carry them in a documentation-only follow-up PR. Never merges over a red or pending check, never launches /code-review, never bounces the dev stack. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-merge))

- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-prepare-docs-reference**: Regenerate docs/reference.md, the annotated index of every document the claude.ai project syncs from this repository, then rebuild docs/claude-transfer/ (the small subset uploaded as project files for mobile) and its own reference.md from the membership file, reporting anything that would make either set misleading. - **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-session-name**: Give the operator the exact /rename command that names the current Claude Code session for the work it holds, so a session list across two machines shows which spec or merge each session belongs to. - **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-sync-local**: Bring this machine up to date after a merge, here or on the other machine, onto the branch the merge landed in (main, or a feature/* branch for a task PR). - **{{skills.prefix}}-fix-process**: Start: a diagnostic is needed, or a warning, error, exception path, broken connection, or measured bottleneck appears. - **{{skills.prefix}}-inline**: Start: a file of the primary language ({{generated.primaryLanguage}}) is edited. End: the edit adds no inline construct that an enabled rule bans, and the project hook accepts it. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/inline) · ★2)

- **{{skills.prefix}}-naming**: Start: a file of the primary language ({{generated.primaryLanguage}}) is created, renamed, or edited, or a symbol is named. End: every new or renamed path and symbol states its domain, responsibility, and result, and every consumer is connected. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/naming) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-stack**: Start: a file of an active language stack changes or a stack check runs. End: the stack's checks pass with error 0 and warn 0 and no forbidden command was used as verification. - **{{skills.prefix}}-structure**: Start: an implementation adds, moves, or renames a file, folder, type, constant, function, or tool module. End: every executed logic is O and every required check reports its exit and counts. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/verify) · ★2)

Тайминги слов — фундамент всего монтажа, на глаз их ставить нельзя. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BE%D0%B7%D0%B2%D1%83%D1%87%D0%BA%D0%B0))
- **рилс-предметы**: Генерирует предметы для кадра в едином стиле, вырезает фон и пополняет библиотеку проекта. Вызывать, когда раскадровка требует предмет, которого нет в библиотеке. Предметы — то, что держит кадр вместо плашек с текстом. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B5%D0%B4%D0%BC%D0%B5%D1%82%D1%8B))
- **рилс-приёмщик**: Находит дефекты до рендера, рендерит ролик и проверяет результат — дыры на пустом холсте, слова не из речи, лист кадров глазами, замер статики, технические параметры файла. Вызывать перед каждым рендером и после него. Без него ролик уходит с браком, который видно зрителю. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B8%D1%91%D0%BC%D1%89%D0%B8%D0%BA))
- **рилс-раскадровщик**: Превращает фразы речи в раскадровку — какая фраза какой сценой показывается, что в кадре и на каком слове появляется. Вызывать после озвучки, до любой вёрстки. Это главный гейт: здесь спорить дёшево, после рендера дорого. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D1%80%D0%B0%D1%81%D0%BA%D0%B0%D0%B4%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%B2%D1%89%D0%B8%D0%BA))
- **рилс-сценарист**: Пишет текст графического рилса под механику сцен — ярлыки, числа вслух, пары «не X, а Y», конкретный призыв.
